<!-- MY TPS - Input Cepat Pemilih & Keluarga -->
<!DOCTYPE html>

<html lang="id"><head><meta charset="utf-8"/><meta content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0, maximum-scale=1.0, user-scalable=no, viewport-fit=cover" name="viewport"/><meta content="mobile_tab" name="shell-type"/><style>@layer base{html,body{width:100vw;margin:0;padding:0;}body{overscroll-behavior:none;}.pb-safe{padding-bottom:env(safe-area-inset-bottom,0px);}.pt-safe{padding-top:env(safe-area-inset-top,0px);}main>:first-child{margin-top:0!important;}main>:last-child{margin-bottom:0!important;}}::-webkit-scrollbar{display:none;}</style><link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Material+Symbols+Outlined:opsz,wght,FILL,GRAD@20..48,100..700,0..1,-50..200" rel="stylesheet"/>
<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Material+Symbols+Outlined:wght,FILL@100..700,0..1&display=swap" rel="stylesheet"/>
<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@100..900&display=swap" rel="stylesheet"/><script src="https://cdn.tailwindcss.com"></script><script id="tailwind-config">tailwind.config = { darkMode: "class", theme: { extend: { colors: { "on-background": "#131b2e", "status-lain-border": "#FECACA", "primary": "#00652c", "status-kita-bg": "#DCFCE7", "surface-border": "#CBD5E1", "on-error-container": "#93000a", "status-lain-bg": "#FEE2E2", "surface-container": "#eaedff", "on-primary": "#ffffff", "on-surface-variant": "#3f493f", "tertiary-fixed": "#ffd9dd", "on-secondary-fixed-variant": "#3a485b", "tertiary-fixed-dim": "#ffb2bd", "on-secondary-fixed": "#0d1c2e", "on-secondary": "#ffffff", "surface-card": "#FFFFFF", "primary-dark": "#166534", "status-kita-text": "#15803D", "surface-container-highest": "#dae2fd", "status-ragu-text": "#B45309", "inverse-surface": "#283044", "on-primary-container": "#d3ffd5", "error": "#ba1a1a", "secondary-container": "#d5e3fc", "secondary": "#515f74", "surface-bright": "#faf8ff", "bg-base": "#F8FAFC", "on-surface": "#131b2e", "surface-variant": "#dae2fd", "surface-tint": "#006d30", "background": "#faf8ff", "secondary-fixed-dim": "#b9c7df", "on-tertiary": "#ffffff", "surface-container-high": "#e2e7ff", "on-secondary-container": "#57657a", "status-ragu-border": "#FDE68A", "tertiary-container": "#b64c62", "inverse-primary": "#79db8d", "on-primary-fixed-variant": "#005323", "on-tertiary-container": "#fff1f1", "primary-fixed-dim": "#79db8d", "tertiary": "#97344a", "on-primary-fixed": "#00210a", "status-ragu-bg": "#FEF3C7", "surface": "#faf8ff", "status-lain-text": "#B91C1C", "inverse-on-surface": "#eef0ff", "surface-dim": "#d2d9f4", "outline-variant": "#becabc", "status-kita-border": "#86EFAC", "primary-fixed": "#95f8a7", "primary-container": "#15803d", "on-error": "#ffffff", "error-container": "#ffdad6", "surface-container-low": "#f2f3ff", "secondary-fixed": "#d5e3fc", "on-tertiary-fixed": "#400013", "outline": "#6f7a6e", "surface-container-lowest": "#ffffff", "on-tertiary-fixed-variant": "#81233b" }, borderRadius: { "DEFAULT": "0.25rem", "lg": "0.5rem", "xl": "0.75rem", "full": "9999px" }, spacing: { "space-sm": "0.5rem", "space-lg": "1rem", "gutter-mobile": "0.75rem", "space-xs": "0.25rem", "margin-mobile": "1rem", "margin": "1.25rem", "gutter": "1rem", "space-md": "0.75rem", "space-xl": "1.5rem" }, fontFamily: { "headline-sm": ["Inter"], "body-sm": ["Inter"], "label-md": ["Inter"], "headline-md": ["Inter"], "label-sm": ["Inter"], "headline-lg-mobile": ["Inter"], "headline-lg": ["Inter"], "body-lg": ["Inter"], "label-lg": ["Inter"], "body-md": ["Inter"] }, fontSize: { "headline-sm": ["18px", { lineHeight: "24px", fontWeight: "600" }], "body-sm": ["14px", { lineHeight: "20px", fontWeight: "400" }], "label-md": ["14px", { lineHeight: "18px", fontWeight: "600" }], "headline-md": ["22px", { lineHeight: "28px", fontWeight: "700" }], "label-sm": ["12px", { lineHeight: "16px", fontWeight: "600" }], "headline-lg-mobile": ["24px", { lineHeight: "32px", fontWeight: "700" }], "headline-lg": ["30px", { lineHeight: "36px", fontWeight: "700" }], "body-lg": ["16px", { lineHeight: "24px", fontWeight: "400" }], "label-lg": ["15px", { lineHeight: "20px", fontWeight: "600" }], "body-md": ["15px", { lineHeight: "22px", fontWeight: "400" }] } } } }</script><style>
    body {
      min-height: max(884px, 100dvh);
    }
  </style>
  </head><body class="bg-surface font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface flex flex-col min-h-screen"><header class="fixed top-0 w-full z-50 pt-safe bg-surface/90 backdrop-blur-xl shadow-[0_1px_8px_rgba(0,0,0,0.04)]"><div class="h-16 px-margin-mobile flex items-center justify-between gap-space-sm"><div class="flex flex-col min-w-0 flex-1"><div class="flex items-center gap-space-xs"><span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-status-kita-text animate-pulse"></span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-status-kita-text uppercase tracking-wide">ONLINE • SINKRON</span></div><h1 class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface truncate leading-tight">Input Cepat Warga</h1></div><div class="flex items-center gap-space-sm shrink-0"><div class="flex flex-col items-end hidden sm:flex"><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface font-bold leading-none">Andi Wijaya</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant text-[11px] leading-tight">Dusun 01 Krajan</span></div><div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-primary flex items-center justify-center"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-on-primary text-[18px]">person</span></div></div></div></header><main class="flex flex-col relative w-full pt-16 pb-24 bg-surface min-h-screen"><div class="flex flex-col w-full pb-28">
<div class="px-margin-mobile py-space-md bg-surface-container-high">
<div class="flex items-center gap-space-xs mb-1">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-primary text-[20px]" style="font-variation-settings: 'FILL' 1;">how_to_reg</span>
<h2 class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface">Catat Dukungan Pemilih</h2>
</div>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface-variant leading-snug">
      Nama Kepala Keluarga (KK) otomatis mengunci untuk seluruh anggota keluarga serumah.
    </p>
</div>
<div class="px-margin-mobile flex flex-col gap-space-lg mt-space-md">
<section class="bg-surface-card rounded-xl p-space-md shadow-sm flex flex-col gap-space-md">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between">
<span class="font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">Identitas Utama Pertemuan</span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm bg-surface-container px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-on-secondary-container">Langkah 1/3</span>
</div>
<div class="flex flex-col gap-1">
<label class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface font-semibold" for="input-nama-utama">Nama Lengkap Pemilih Utama *</label>
<input class="w-full h-11 px-3 bg-surface rounded-lg font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary shadow-sm" id="input-nama-utama" oninput="syncKepalaKeluarga(this.value)" placeholder="Contoh: Ahmad Supardi" type="text"/>
</div>
<div class="flex flex-col gap-1">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between">
<label class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface font-semibold" for="input-nama-kk">Nama Kepala Keluarga (KK)</label>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-primary flex items-center gap-0.5">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[14px]">lock</span> Terkunci KK
          </span>
</div>
<div class="relative">
<input class="w-full h-11 px-3 pr-10 bg-surface-container-low rounded-lg font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary shadow-sm" id="input-nama-kk" placeholder="Sama dengan nama pemilih utama" type="text"/>
<button class="absolute right-2 top-2.5 text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface" onclick="document.getElementById('input-nama-kk').focus()" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]">edit</span>
</button>
</div>
</div>
<div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-space-sm">
<div class="flex flex-col gap-1">
<label class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface font-semibold">Dusun (Terkunci)</label>
<div class="h-11 px-3 bg-surface-container-low rounded-lg flex items-center gap-1.5 text-on-surface-variant">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]">pin_drop</span>
<span class="font-body-sm text-body-sm truncate font-medium">Dusun 01 Krajan</span>
</div>
</div>
<div class="flex flex-col gap-1">
<label class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface font-semibold" for="select-rt">RT Wilayah *</label>
<select class="w-full h-11 px-3 bg-surface rounded-lg font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary shadow-sm" id="select-rt">
<option value="RT 01">RT 01</option>
<option value="RT 02">RT 02</option>
<option value="RT 03">RT 03</option>
</select>
</div>
</div>
<div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-space-sm">
<div class="flex flex-col gap-1">
<label class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface font-semibold" for="input-wa">Nomor WhatsApp</label>
<input class="w-full h-11 px-3 bg-surface rounded-lg font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary shadow-sm" id="input-wa" placeholder="08xxxxxxxxxx" type="tel"/>
</div>
<div class="flex flex-col gap-1">
<label class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface font-semibold" for="select-tps">Ploting TPS</label>
<select class="w-full h-11 px-3 bg-surface rounded-lg font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary shadow-sm" id="select-tps">
<option value="TPS 01 Krajan">TPS 01 Krajan</option>
<option value="TPS 02 Krajan">TPS 02 Krajan</option>
<option value="TPS 03 Krajan">TPS 03 Krajan</option>
</select>
</div>
</div>
</section>
<section class="bg-surface-card rounded-xl p-space-md shadow-sm flex flex-col gap-space-md">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between">
<span class="font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">Arah Dukungan Pemilih Utama</span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-primary font-bold">1-Klik Cepat</span>
</div>
<div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-2.5">
<button class="min-h-[52px] w-full px-4 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-between transition-all bg-status-kita-bg text-status-kita-text shadow-sm" id="btn-utama-hijau" onclick="setPilihanUtama('hijau')" type="button">
<div class="flex items-center gap-3">
<span class="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-primary-container text-on-primary flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-xs">A</span>
<div class="flex flex-col text-left">
<span class="font-label-md text-label-md font-bold">HIJAU (Pasti Haji Rohmat)</span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm opacity-90">Pemilih loyal & siap coblos nomor 01</span>
</div>
</div>
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]" id="check-utama-hijau">check_circle</span>
</button>
<button class="min-h-[52px] w-full px-4 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-between transition-all bg-surface text-on-surface-variant opacity-85 shadow-sm" id="btn-utama-kuning" onclick="setPilihanUtama('kuning')" type="button">
<div class="flex items-center gap-3">
<span class="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-status-ragu-bg text-status-ragu-text flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-xs">B</span>
<div class="flex flex-col text-left">
<span class="font-label-md text-label-md font-bold">KUNING (Masih Ragu / Swing)</span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm opacity-80">Perlu dikunjungi ulang oleh tokoh</span>
</div>
</div>
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px] opacity-20" id="check-utama-kuning">radio_button_unchecked</span>
</button>
<button class="min-h-[52px] w-full px-4 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-between transition-all bg-surface text-on-surface-variant opacity-85 shadow-sm" id="btn-utama-merah" onclick="setPilihanUtama('merah')" type="button">
<div class="flex items-center gap-3">
<span class="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-status-lain-bg text-status-lain-text flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-xs">C</span>
<div class="flex flex-col text-left">
<span class="font-label-md text-label-md font-bold">MERAH (Pilih Calon Lawan)</span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm opacity-80">Dukungan mengarah ke paslon lain</span>
</div>
</div>
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px] opacity-20" id="check-utama-merah">radio_button_unchecked</span>
</button>
</div>
<div class="hidden flex-col gap-1 p-3 bg-status-lain-bg rounded-lg" id="dropdown-lawan-utama">
<label class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-status-lain-text font-bold" for="select-lawan-utama">Identifikasi Pilihan Lawan:</label>
<select class="w-full h-11 px-3 bg-surface-card rounded-lg font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-error shadow-sm" id="select-lawan-utama">
<option value="No. 02 Drs. Bambang">No. 02 Drs. Bambang</option>
<option value="No. 03 Hj. Ratna Sari">No. 03 Hj. Ratna Sari</option>
<option value="Dirahasiakan / Tidak Mau Sebut">Dirahasiakan / Tidak Mau Sebut</option>
</select>
</div>
</section>
<section class="bg-surface-card rounded-xl p-space-md shadow-sm flex flex-col gap-space-md">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between">
<div class="flex flex-col">
<span class="font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface font-bold">Anggota Keluarga Satu KK</span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant">Otomatis mewarisi KK, RT & TPS</span>
</div>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm bg-primary text-on-primary px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-bold" id="badge-total-keluarga">1 Orang</span>
</div>
<div class="flex flex-col gap-space-sm" id="container-keluarga"></div>
<button class="min-h-[46px] w-full bg-surface-container hover:bg-surface-container-highest text-primary font-label-md text-label-md font-bold rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition-all" onclick="tambahAnggotaKeluarga()" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]">group_add</span>
        + Tambah Anggota Keluarga Serumah (Istri/Anak)
      </button>
</section>
<section class="bg-surface-card rounded-xl p-space-md shadow-sm flex flex-col gap-1.5">
<label class="font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface font-bold" for="catatan-lapangan">Catatan Lapangan Lapangan Khusus</label>
<textarea class="w-full p-3 bg-surface rounded-lg font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary shadow-sm resize-none" id="catatan-lapangan" placeholder="Contoh: Minta dikunjungi ulang minggu depan / Perlu jemputan kendaraan saat pencoblosan." rows="3"></textarea>
</section>
</div>
<div class="fixed bottom-16 left-0 right-0 z-40 p-margin-mobile bg-surface/90 backdrop-blur-md">
<button class="w-full h-[50px] bg-primary-container active:bg-primary-dark text-on-primary font-headline-sm text-headline-sm rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-lg transition-transform active:scale-[0.98]" onclick="simpanData()" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">save</span>
      Simpan Data Pemilih & Keluarga
    </button>
</div>
<div class="fixed top-20 left-4 right-4 z-50 transform -translate-y-24 transition-transform duration-300 pointer-events-none" id="toast-sukses">
<div class="bg-status-kita-bg text-status-kita-text p-4 rounded-xl shadow-lg flex items-center gap-3">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[26px]">task_alt</span>
<div class="flex flex-col">
<span class="font-label-md text-label-md font-bold">Data Berhasil Disimpan!</span>
<span class="font-body-sm text-body-sm">Dukungan satu keluarga tersinkron otomatis ke server.</span>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<script>
  let pilihanUtama = 'hijau';
  let jumlahKeluarga = 0;
  const anggotaKeluarga = [];

  function syncKepalaKeluarga(val) {
    const inputKk = document.getElementById('input-nama-kk');
    if (!inputKk.dataset.manualEdited) {
      inputKk.value = val;
    }
  }

  document.getElementById('input-nama-kk').addEventListener('input', function() {
    this.dataset.manualEdited = "true";
  });

  function setPilihanUtama(warna) {
    pilihanUtama = warna;
    const btnHijau = document.getElementById('btn-utama-hijau');
    const btnKuning = document.getElementById('btn-utama-kuning');
    const btnMerah = document.getElementById('btn-utama-merah');
    const checkHijau = document.getElementById('check-utama-hijau');
    const checkKuning = document.getElementById('check-utama-kuning');
    const checkMerah = document.getElementById('check-utama-merah');
    const dropdownLawan = document.getElementById('dropdown-lawan-utama');

    btnHijau.className = "min-h-[52px] w-full px-4 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-between transition-all shadow-sm bg-surface text-on-surface-variant opacity-80";
    btnKuning.className = "min-h-[52px] w-full px-4 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-between transition-all shadow-sm bg-surface text-on-surface-variant opacity-80";
    btnMerah.className = "min-h-[52px] w-full px-4 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-between transition-all shadow-sm bg-surface text-on-surface-variant opacity-80";

    checkHijau.textContent = "radio_button_unchecked";
    checkHijau.className = "material-symbols-outlined text-[24px] opacity-30";
    checkKuning.textContent = "radio_button_unchecked";
    checkKuning.className = "material-symbols-outlined text-[24px] opacity-30";
    checkMerah.textContent = "radio_button_unchecked";
    checkMerah.className = "material-symbols-outlined text-[24px] opacity-30";

    dropdownLawan.classList.add('hidden');

    if (warna === 'hijau') {
      btnHijau.className = "min-h-[52px] w-full px-4 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-between transition-all shadow-sm bg-status-kita-bg text-status-kita-text";
      checkHijau.textContent = "check_circle";
      checkHijau.className = "material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]";
    } else if (warna === 'kuning') {
      btnKuning.className = "min-h-[52px] w-full px-4 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-between transition-all shadow-sm bg-status-ragu-bg text-status-ragu-text";
      checkKuning.textContent = "check_circle";
      checkKuning.className = "material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]";
    } else if (warna === 'merah') {
      btnMerah.className = "min-h-[52px] w-full px-4 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-between transition-all shadow-sm bg-status-lain-bg text-status-lain-text";
      checkMerah.textContent = "check_circle";
      checkMerah.className = "material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]";
      dropdownLawan.classList.remove('hidden');
    }
  }

  function tambahAnggotaKeluarga() {
    jumlahKeluarga++;
    const id = 'anggota-' + Date.now();
    const container = document.getElementById('container-keluarga');

    const card = document.createElement('div');
    card.id = id;
    card.className = "p-3 bg-surface-container-low rounded-xl flex flex-col gap-2 relative shadow-sm";
    
    card.innerHTML = `
      <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
        <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <span class="w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-secondary text-on-secondary flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-[11px]">${container.children.length + 1}</span>
          <span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface font-bold">Anggota Tambahan (Satu KK)</span>
        </div>
        <button type="button" onclick="hapusAnggota('${id}')" class="text-error flex items-center p-1">
          <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]">delete</span>
        </button>
      </div>
      <div class="flex gap-2">
        <input type="text" placeholder="Nama Istri / Anak" class="w-full h-10 px-3 bg-surface-card rounded-lg font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-primary shadow-sm" />
        <select class="h-10 px-2 bg-surface-card rounded-lg font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface focus:outline-none shadow-sm">
          <option>Istri</option>
          <option>Anak</option>
          <option>Orang Tua</option>
          <option>Famili Lain</option>
        </select>
      </div>
      <div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-1.5 pt-1">
        <button type="button" onclick="setPilihanAnggota('${id}', 'hijau')" class="btn-pil-hijau h-9 rounded-lg bg-status-kita-bg text-status-kita-text font-label-sm text-label-sm font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-1 shadow-sm">
          <span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-status-kita-text"></span> Hijau
        </button>
        <button type="button" onclick="setPilihanAnggota('${id}', 'kuning')" class="btn-pil-kuning h-9 rounded-lg bg-surface-card text-on-surface-variant font-label-sm text-label-sm opacity-75 flex items-center justify-center gap-1 shadow-sm">
          <span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-status-ragu-text"></span> Kuning
        </button>
        <button type="button" onclick="setPilihanAnggota('${id}', 'merah')" class="btn-pil-merah h-9 rounded-lg bg-surface-card text-on-surface-variant font-label-sm text-label-sm opacity-75 flex items-center justify-center gap-1 shadow-sm">
          <span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-status-lain-text"></span> Merah
        </button>
      </div>
    `;

    container.appendChild(card);
    updateTotalKeluarga();
  }

  function hapusAnggota(id) {
    const el = document.getElementById(id);
    if (el) {
      el.remove();
      updateTotalKeluarga();
    }
  }

  function updateTotalKeluarga() {
    const count = document.getElementById('container-keluarga').children.length + 1;
    document.getElementById('badge-total-keluarga').textContent = count + ' Orang';
  }

  function setPilihanAnggota(cardId, warna) {
    const card = document.getElementById(cardId);
    if (!card) return;

    const bHijau = card.querySelector('.btn-pil-hijau');
    const bKuning = card.querySelector('.btn-pil-kuning');
    const bMerah = card.querySelector('.btn-pil-merah');

    bHijau.className = "btn-pil-hijau h-9 rounded-lg bg-surface-card text-on-surface-variant font-label-sm text-label-sm opacity-75 flex items-center justify-center gap-1 shadow-sm";
    bKuning.className = "btn-pil-kuning h-9 rounded-lg bg-surface-card text-on-surface-variant font-label-sm text-label-sm opacity-75 flex items-center justify-center gap-1 shadow-sm";
    bMerah.className = "btn-pil-merah h-9 rounded-lg bg-surface-card text-on-surface-variant font-label-sm text-label-sm opacity-75 flex items-center justify-center gap-1 shadow-sm";

    if (warna === 'hijau') {
      bHijau.className = "btn-pil-hijau h-9 rounded-lg bg-status-kita-bg text-status-kita-text font-label-sm text-label-sm font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-1 shadow-sm";
    } else if (warna === 'kuning') {
      bKuning.className = "btn-pil-kuning h-9 rounded-lg bg-status-ragu-bg text-status-ragu-text font-label-sm text-label-sm font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-1 shadow-sm";
    } else if (warna === 'merah') {
      bMerah.className = "btn-pil-merah h-9 rounded-lg bg-status-lain-bg text-status-lain-text font-label-sm text-label-sm font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-1 shadow-sm";
    }
  }

  function simpanData() {
    const namaUtama = document.getElementById('input-nama-utama').value.trim();
    if (!namaUtama) {
      document.getElementById('input-nama-utama').focus();
      return;
    }

    const toast = document.getElementById('toast-sukses');
    toast.classList.remove('-translate-y-24');
    toast.classList.add('translate-y-0');

    setTimeout(() => {
      toast.classList.remove('translate-y-0');
      toast.classList.add('-translate-y-24');
      document.getElementById('input-nama-utama').value = '';
      document.getElementById('input-nama-kk').value = '';
      document.getElementById('input-wa').value = '';
      document.getElementById('catatan-lapangan').value = '';
      document.getElementById('container-keluarga').innerHTML = '';
      setPilihanUtama('hijau');
      updateTotalKeluarga();
    }, 1800);
  }
</script></main><nav class="fixed bottom-0 w-full z-50 pb-safe bg-surface/95 backdrop-blur-xl shadow-[0_-2px_12px_rgba(0,0,0,0.06)]" data-active-classes="text-primary font-label-md"><div class="flex items-center justify-around h-16 px-space-xs"><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-w-[64px] min-h-[44px] py-1 text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface transition-colors" data-path="beranda-setoran" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">how_to_vote</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm mt-0.5">Setoran</span></a><a aria-current="page" class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-w-[64px] min-h-[44px] py-1 transition-colors text-primary font-label-md" data-path="input-cepat-warga" href="#"><div class="w-10 h-10 -mt-3 rounded-full bg-primary-container text-on-primary flex items-center justify-center shadow-[0_2px_8px_rgba(21,128,61,0.35)]"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">person_add</span></div><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm mt-0.5">Input</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-w-[64px] min-h-[44px] py-1 text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface transition-colors" data-path="ploting-target" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">ads_click</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm mt-0.5">Target</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-w-[64px] min-h-[44px] py-1 text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface transition-colors" data-path="akun-timses" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">badge</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm mt-0.5">Akun</span></a></div></nav></body></html>

</div>
<button class="min-h-[44px] px-4 mt-space-xs bg-primary text-on-primary rounded-lg font-label-md text-label-md active:bg-primary-dark transition-colors" id="resetFilterBtn" type="button">

      </button>

<script>
  (function() {
    const searchInput = document.getElementById('voterSearchInput');
    const tabs = document.querySelectorAll('.filter-tab');
    const cards = document.querySelectorAll('.voter-card');
    const emptyState = document.getElementById('voterEmptyState');
    const resetBtn = document.getElementById('resetFilterBtn');

    let currentFilter = 'all';

    function applyFiltering() {
      const query = (searchInput.value || '').toLowerCase().trim();
      let visibleCount = 0;

      cards.forEach(card => {
        const cardStatus = card.getAttribute('data-status');
        const cardSearch = (card.getAttribute('data-search') || '').toLowerCase();

        const matchesStatus = (currentFilter === 'all' || cardStatus === currentFilter);
        const matchesQuery = (!query || cardSearch.includes(query));

        if (matchesStatus && matchesQuery) {
          card.classList.remove('hidden');
          card.classList.add('flex');
          visibleCount++;
        } else {
          card.classList.remove('flex');
          card.classList.add('hidden');
        }
      });

      if (visibleCount === 0) {
        emptyState.classList.remove('hidden');
        emptyState.classList.add('flex');
      } else {
        emptyState.classList.remove('flex');
        emptyState.classList.add('hidden');
      }
    }

    tabs.forEach(tab => {
      tab.addEventListener('click', function() {
        tabs.forEach(t => {
          t.classList.remove('bg-primary', 'text-on-primary');
          t.classList.add('bg-surface-card');
          if (t.dataset.filter === 'hijau') t.classList.add('text-status-kita-text');
          if (t.dataset.filter === 'kuning') t.classList.add('text-status-ragu-text');
          if (t.dataset.filter === 'merah') t.classList.add('text-status-lain-text');
          if (t.dataset.filter === 'all') t.classList.add('text-on-surface');
        });

        this.classList.remove('bg-surface-card', 'text-status-kita-text', 'text-status-ragu-text', 'text-status-lain-text', 'text-on-surface');
        this.classList.add('bg-primary', 'text-on-primary');

        currentFilter = this.getAttribute('data-filter');
        applyFiltering();
      });
    });

    if (searchInput) {
      searchInput.addEventListener('input', applyFiltering);
    }

    if (resetBtn) {
      resetBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
        searchInput.value = '';
        const allTab = document.querySelector('[data-filter="all"]');
        if (allTab) allTab.click();
      });
    }
  })();
</script></main><nav class="fixed bottom-0 w-full z-50 pb-safe bg-surface/95 backdrop-blur-xl shadow-[0_-2px_12px_rgba(0,0,0,0.06)]" data-active-classes="text-primary font-label-md"><div class="flex items-center justify-around h-16 px-space-xs"><a aria-current="page" class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-w-[64px] min-h-[44px] py-1 transition-colors text-primary font-label-md" data-path="beranda-setoran" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">how_to_vote</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm mt-0.5">Setoran</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-w-[64px] min-h-[44px] py-1 text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface transition-colors" data-path="input-cepat-warga" href="#"><div class="w-10 h-10 -mt-3 rounded-full bg-primary-container text-on-primary flex items-center justify-center shadow-[0_2px_8px_rgba(21,128,61,0.35)]"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">person_add</span></div><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm mt-0.5">Input</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-w-[64px] min-h-[44px] py-1 text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface transition-colors" data-path="ploting-target" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">ads_click</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm mt-0.5">Target</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-w-[64px] min-h-[44px] py-1 text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface transition-colors" data-path="akun-timses" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">badge</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm mt-0.5">Akun</span></a></div></nav></body></html>

<!DOCTYPE html>

<html lang="id" style=""><head>

<script id="tailwind-config">
    tailwind.config = {
      darkMode: "class",
      theme: {
        extend: {
          colors: {
            "primary": "#15803d",
            "primary-dark": "#166534",
            "primary-container": "#dcfce7",
            "on-primary": "#ffffff",
            "surface": "#F8FAFC",
            "surface-card": "#FFFFFF",
            "surface-border": "#CBD5E1",
            "on-surface": "#0f172a",
            "on-surface-variant": "#475569",
            "status-kita-bg": "#DCFCE7",
            "status-kita-text": "#15803D",
            "status-kita-border": "#86EFAC"
          },
          fontFamily: {
            sans: ["Inter", "sans-serif"],
            mono: ["JetBrains Mono", "monospace"]
          }
        }
      }
    };
  </script>
<style>
    @layer base {
      html, body {
        width: 100%;
        margin: 0;
        padding: 0;
        min-height: 100%;
        font-family: 'Inter', sans-serif;

      }
      body {
        overscroll-behavior: none;
      }
      .pb-safe {
        padding-bottom: env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 16px);
      }
      .pt-safe {
        padding-top: env(safe-area-inset-top, 16px);
      }

    ::-webkit-scrollbar {
      display: none;
    }
  </style>
</head>
<body class="bg-surface font-sans text-on-surface flex flex-col min-h-screen antialiased selection:bg-emerald-100">
<main class="flex-1 flex flex-col relative w-full max-w-md mx-auto pt-safe pb-safe px-4 bg-surface">

<span class="animate-ping absolute inline-flex h-full w-full rounded-full bg-emerald-500 opacity-75"></span>

</span>

</div>
</div>
<!-- Main Instagram-style Card -->
<div class="bg-surface-card border border-slate-200 rounded-3xl p-6 shadow-sm flex flex-col items-center mb-4">
<!-- Logo & Clean Branding (Instagram vibe) -->
<div class="flex flex-col items-center text-center mb-7 w-full">
<div class="w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl bg-primary flex items-center justify-center shadow-md mb-3">

</div>
<h1 class="text-2xl font-black text-slate-900 tracking-tight leading-none mb-1.5">MY TPS</h1>
<div class="inline-block text-[11px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-widest text-primary bg-emerald-50 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full mb-1">TIMSES HAJI ROHMAT</div>
<p class="text-xs text-slate-500 font-medium">Sistem Pengawalan &amp; Tabulasi Suara Pilkades</p>
</div>
<!-- Form Input Ala Instagram -->
<form class="w-full flex flex-col gap-3" onsubmit="handleLogin(event)">
<div class="relative flex items-center">
<input class="w-full min-h-[48px] px-3.5 py-3 bg-slate-50/70 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl font-medium text-sm text-slate-900 placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary focus:border-transparent focus:bg-white transition-all" id="login-id" placeholder="Nomor WhatsApp atau ID Timses" required="" type="text"/>
<button class="absolute right-3 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 p-1" onclick="clearInput('login-id')" title="Hapus input" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]">cancel</span>
</button>
</div>
<div class="relative flex items-center">
<input class="w-full min-h-[48px] pl-3.5 pr-11 py-3 bg-slate-50/70 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl font-medium text-sm text-slate-900 placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary focus:border-transparent focus:bg-white transition-all" id="login-password" placeholder="Kata Sandi Akun" required="" type="password"/>
<button class="absolute right-3 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 p-1 flex items-center" onclick="togglePassVisibility('login-password', 'pass-icon')" title="Tampilkan sandi" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]" id="pass-icon">visibility</span>
</button>
</div>
<!-- Main Submit Button -->
<button class="w-full min-h-[48px] h-12 bg-primary hover:bg-primary-dark active:bg-emerald-900 text-white rounded-xl font-bold text-sm flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition-all shadow-md shadow-emerald-900/15 tracking-wide mt-1" id="btn-login-main" type="submit">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]">login</span>
<span>Masuk Aplikasi</span>
</button>
</form>
<!-- Separator Instagram (Garis Tipis 'ATAU') -->
<div class="relative flex py-5 items-center w-full">
<div class="flex-grow border-t border-slate-200"></div>
<span class="flex-shrink mx-3 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest">ATAU</span>
<div class="flex-grow border-t border-slate-200"></div>
</div>
<!-- Google Login Button -->
<button class="w-full min-h-[48px] h-12 bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 border border-slate-300 text-slate-800 rounded-xl font-bold text-xs flex items-center justify-center gap-3 active:bg-slate-100 px-3 shadow-2xs transition-all" onclick="handleGoogleLogin()" type="button">
<svg aria-hidden="true" class="w-5 h-5 shrink-0" viewbox="0 0 24 24">
<path d="M22.56 12.25c0-.78-.07-1.53-.2-2.25H12v4.26h5.92c-.26 1.37-1.04 2.53-2.21 3.31v2.77h3.57c2.08-1.92 3.28-4.74 3.28-8.09z" fill="#4285F4"></path>
<path d="M12 23c2.97 0 5.46-.98 7.28-2.66l-3.57-2.77c-.98.66-2.23 1.06-3.71 1.06-2.86 0-5.29-1.93-6.16-4.53H2.18v2.84C3.99 20.53 7.7 23 12 23z" fill="#34A853"></path>
<path d="M5.84 14.09c-.22-.66-.35-1.36-.35-2.09s.13-1.43.35-2.09V7.06H2.18C1.43 8.55 1 10.22 1 12s.43 3.45 1.18 4.94l2.85-2.22.81-.63z" fill="#FBBC05"></path>
<path d="M12 5.38c1.62 0 3.06.56 4.21 1.64l3.15-3.15C17.45 2.09 14.97 1 12 1 7.7 1 3.99 3.47 2.18 7.06l3.66 2.84c.87-2.6 3.3-4.52 6.16-4.52z" fill="#EA4335"></path>
</svg>
<span class="tracking-wide">Masuk Cepat dengan Akun Google</span>
</button>
<!-- Lupa Sandi Hubungi WhatsApp -->
<div class="mt-4 pt-1">
<a class="text-xs font-semibold text-primary hover:underline inline-flex items-center gap-1" href="https://wa.me/6281234567890?text=Halo%20Admin%20MY%20TPS%2C%20saya%20lupa%20password" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">
<span>Lupa kata sandi? Hubungi Admin via WhatsApp</span>
</a>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Bottom Card ala Instagram (Box Terpisah di Bawah) -->
<div class="bg-surface-card border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-4 shadow-xs text-center flex flex-col items-center gap-1 mb-4">
<div class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-slate-600 text-xs font-semibold">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] text-primary">verified_user</span>
<span>Aplikasi Internal Pemenangan Pilkades</span>
</div>
<p class="text-[11px] text-slate-500 font-medium">© 2024 Timses H. Rahmat Hidayat • Kampung Makmur Jaya</p>
</div>
<!-- Offline Ready Badge -->
<div class="flex justify-center mb-2">
<div class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-slate-500 text-[11px] bg-slate-100/90 px-3 py-1 rounded-full border border-slate-200">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[15px] text-emerald-700">wifi_off</span>
<span class="font-medium">Mode Offline-First aktif tanpa sinyal internet</span>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Feedback Toast -->
<div class="hidden fixed bottom-6 left-4 right-4 max-w-md mx-auto p-3.5 bg-slate-900 text-white rounded-2xl flex items-center gap-3 z-50 shadow-xl border border-slate-700" id="login-feedback">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-emerald-400 text-[20px]" id="feedback-icon">check_circle</span>
<span class="text-xs font-medium flex-1 text-slate-100" id="feedback-text">Memproses verifikasi...</span>
</div></div>
</main>
<script>
    function clearInput(id) {
      const el = document.getElementById(id);
      if (el) {
        el.value = '';
        el.focus();
      }
    }

    function togglePassVisibility(inputId, iconId) {
      const input = document.getElementById(inputId);
      const icon = document.getElementById(iconId);
      if (!input || !icon) return;
      if (input.type === 'password') {
        input.type = 'text';
        icon.textContent = 'visibility_off';
      } else {
        input.type = 'password';
        icon.textContent = 'visibility';
      }
    }

    function showToast(message, isError = false) {
      const toast = document.getElementById('login-feedback');
      const icon = document.getElementById('feedback-icon');
      const text = document.getElementById('feedback-text');

      text.textContent = message;
      icon.textContent = isError ? 'error' : 'check_circle';
      icon.className = isError 
        ? 'material-symbols-outlined text-rose-400 text-[20px]' 
        : 'material-symbols-outlined text-emerald-400 text-[20px]';

      toast.classList.remove('hidden');
      setTimeout(() => {
        toast.classList.add('hidden');
      }, 3200);
    }

    function handleLogin(event) {
      event.preventDefault();
      const loginId = document.getElementById('login-id').value.trim();
      const pass = document.getElementById('login-password').value.trim();
      const btn = document.getElementById('btn-login-main');

      if (!loginId) {
        showToast('Masukkan ID Pengguna atau Nomor WhatsApp', true);
        return;
      }
      if (!pass) {
        showToast('Kata sandi wajib diisi', true);
        return;
      }

      btn.disabled = true;
      btn.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined animate-spin text-[18px]">progress_activity</span><span>Memverifikasi Akun Lapangan...</span>';

      setTimeout(() => {
        showToast('Akses Diterima! Selamat bertugas Timses Haji Rohmat.');
        setTimeout(() => {
          btn.disabled = false;
          btn.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]">login</span><span>Masuk Aplikasi</span>';
        }, 1200);
      }, 900);
    }

    function handleGoogleLogin() {
      showToast('Menghubungkan Akun Google di perangkat...');
      setTimeout(() => {
        showToast('Verifikasi akun Google pengurus sukses! Membuka portal...');
      }, 1100);
    }
  </script>
</body></html>

"label-sm": ["Inter"], "headline-lg-mobile": ["Inter"], "headline-lg": ["Inter"], "body-lg": ["Inter"], "label-lg": ["Inter"], "body-md": ["Inter"] }, fontSize: { "headline-sm": ["18px", { lineHeight: "24px", fontWeight: "600" }], "body-sm": ["14px", { lineHeight: "20px", fontWeight: "400" }], "label-md": ["14px", { lineHeight: "18px", fontWeight: "600" }], "headline-md": ["22px", { lineHeight: "28px", fontWeight: "700" }], "label-sm": ["12px", { lineHeight: "16px", fontWeight: "600" }], "headline-lg-mobile": ["24px", { lineHeight: "32px", fontWeight: "700" }], "headline-lg": ["30px", { </head><body class="bg-surface font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface flex flex-col min-h-screen"><header class="fixed top-0 w-full z-50 pt-safe bg-surface/90 backdrop-blur-xl shadow-[0_1px_8px_rgba(0,0,0,0.04)]"><div class="h-14 px-4 flex items-center justify-between border-b border-surface-border/40 bg-surface-card"><button class="w-9 h-9 flex items-center justify-center text-on-surface hover:text-primary transition-colors" type="button"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">close</span></button><h1 class="font-label-lg text-label-lg font-bold text-on-surface text-center">Input Pemilih</h1><button class="text-primary font-label-md text-label-md font-bold px-2 py-1 hover:opacity-80 transition-opacity flex items-center gap-1" onclick="simpanData()" type="button"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]">check</span><span class="hidden sm:inline">Simpan</span></button></div></header><main class="flex flex-col relative w-full pb-24 bg-surface min-h-screen pt-14"><div class="flex flex-col w-full pb-28"><div class="px-4 py-4 flex flex-col gap-4 max-w-lg mx-auto w-full"><section class="bg-surface-card rounded-2xl p-4 shadow-sm border border-surface-border/30 flex flex-col gap-3.5"><div class="flex flex-col gap-1"><label class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant font-medium" for="input-nama-utama">Nama Pemilih *</label><input class="w-full h-11 px-3 bg-surface-container-low rounded-xl font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-primary" id="input-nama-utama" oninput="syncKepalaKeluarga(this.value)" placeholder="Nama lengkap" type="text"/></div><div class="flex flex-col gap-1"><div class="flex items-center justify-between"><label class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant font-medium" for="input-nama-kk">Kepala Keluarga (KK)</label><span class="font-label-sm text-[11px] text-primary flex items-center gap-0.5 font-semibold"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[12px]">lock</span> Terkunci KK</span></div><div class="relative"><input class="w-full h-11 px-3 pr-9 bg-surface-container-low rounded-xl font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-primary" id="input-nama-kk" placeholder="Sama dengan pemilih utama" type="text"/><button class="absolute right-2.5 top-3 text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface" onclick="document.getElementById('input-nama-kk').focus()" type="button"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]">edit</span></button></div></div><div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3"><div class="flex flex-col gap-1"><label class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant font-medium">Dusun</label><div class="h-11 px-3 bg-surface-container-low rounded-xl flex items-center gap-1.5 text-on-surface-variant"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] text-primary">pin_drop</span><span class="font-body-sm text-body-sm truncate font-medium text-on-surface">01 Krajan</span></div></div><div class="flex flex-col gap-1"><label class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant font-medium" for="select-rt">RT *</label><div class="relative"><select class="w-full h-11 px-3 bg-surface-container-low rounded-xl font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-primary appearance-none" id="select-rt"><option value="RT 01">RT 01</option><option value="RT 02">RT 02</option><option value="RT 03">RT 03</option></select><span class="material-symbols-outlined absolute right-2.5 top-3 pointer-events-none text-[18px] text-on-surface-variant">expand_more</span></div></div></div><div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3"><div class="flex flex-col gap-1"><label class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant font-medium" for="input-wa">No. WhatsApp</label><div class="relative"><input class="w-full h-11 pl-8 pr-3 bg-surface-container-low rounded-xl font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-primary" id="input-wa" placeholder="08..." type="tel"/><span class="material-symbols-outlined absolute left-2.5 top-3 pointer-events-none text-[16px] text-on-surface-variant">call</span></div></div><div class="flex flex-col gap-1"><label class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant font-medium" for="select-tps">TPS</label><div class="relative"><select class="w-full h-11 px-3 bg-surface-container-low rounded-xl font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-primary appearance-none" id="select-tps"><option value="TPS 01 Krajan">TPS 01</option><option value="TPS 02 Krajan">TPS 02</option><option value="TPS 03 Krajan">TPS 03</option></select><span class="material-symbols-outlined absolute right-2.5 top-3 pointer-events-none text-[18px] text-on-surface-variant">expand_more</span></div></div></div></section><section class="bg-surface-card rounded-2xl p-4 shadow-sm border border-surface-border/30 flex flex-col gap-3"><div class="flex items-center justify-between"><span class="font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface font-bold">Status Dukungan</span><span class="text-[11px] text-on-surface-variant font-medium uppercase tracking-wider">1-Tap</span></div><div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2"><button class="h-12 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 transition-all bg-status-kita-bg text-status-kita-text font-bold text-label-sm shadow-sm" id="btn-utama-hijau" onclick="setPilihanUtama('hijau')" type="button"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]" id="check-utama-hijau">check_circle</span><span>Hijau</span></button><button class="h-12 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 transition-all bg-surface-container-low text-on-surface-variant font-semibold text-label-sm" id="btn-utama-kuning" onclick="setPilihanUtama('kuning')" type="button"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px] opacity-40" id="check-utama-kuning">help</span><span>Ragu</span></button><button class="h-12 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 transition-all bg-surface-container-low text-on-surface-variant font-semibold text-label-sm" id="btn-utama-merah" onclick="setPilihanUtama('merah')" type="button"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px] opacity-40" id="check-utama-merah">cancel</span><span>Lawan</span></button></div><div class="hidden flex-col gap-1 p-3 bg-status-lain-bg/60 rounded-xl" id="dropdown-lawan-utama"><label class="font-label-sm text-[12px] text-status-lain-text font-semibold" for="select-lawan-utama">Arah Lawan:</label><select class="w-full h-10 px-3 bg-surface-card rounded-lg font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-error" id="select-lawan-utama"><option value="No. 02 Drs. Bambang">No. 02 Drs. Bambang</option><option value="No. 03 Hj. Ratna Sari">No. 03 Hj. Ratna Sari</option><option value="Dirahasiakan / Tidak Mau Sebut">Dirahasiakan / Tidak Mau Sebut</option></select></div></section><section class="bg-surface-card rounded-2xl p-4 shadow-sm border border-surface-border/30 flex flex-col gap-3"><div class="flex items-center justify-between"><div class="flex items-center gap-1.5"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px] text-primary">group</span><span class="font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface font-bold">Anggota Serumah</span></div><span class="font-label-sm text-[11px] bg-surface-container px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-bold text-on-surface-variant" id="badge-total-keluarga">1 Orang</span></div><div class="flex flex-col gap-2" id="container-keluarga"></div><button class="h-10 w-full bg-surface-container-low hover:bg-surface-container text-primary font-label-sm text-label-sm font-bold rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 transition-all" onclick="tambahAnggotaKeluarga()" type="button"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]">person_add</span>+ Tambah Anggota</button></section><section class="bg-surface-card rounded-2xl p-4 shadow-sm border border-surface-border/30 flex flex-col gap-2"><label class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant font-medium" for="catatan-lapangan">Catatan Khusus</label><textarea class="w-full p-3 bg-surface-container-low rounded-xl font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-primary resize-none" id="catatan-lapangan" placeholder="Tulis catatan kunjungan..." rows="2"></textarea></section></div><div class="fixed bottom-16 left-0 right-0 z-40 p-4 bg-surface/90 backdrop-blur-md max-w-lg mx-auto"><button class="w-full h-12 bg-primary active:bg-primary-dark text-on-primary font-label-md text-label-md font-bold rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-sm transition-transform active:scale-[0.98]" onclick="simpanData()" type="button"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]">check</span>Simpan Data Pemilih</button></div><div class="fixed top-16 left-4 right-4 z-50 transform -translate-y-24 transition-transform duration-300 pointer-events-none max-w-md mx-auto" id="toast-sukses"><div class="bg-status-kita-bg text-status-kita-text p-3.5 rounded-xl shadow-lg flex items-center gap-2.5 border border-status-kita-border"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">task_alt</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm font-bold">Data pemilih berhasil disimpan</span></div></div></div>
<script>
  let pilihanUtama = 'hijau';
  let jumlahKeluarga = 0;
  const anggotaKeluarga = [];

  function syncKepalaKeluarga(val) {
    const inputKk = document.getElementById('input-nama-kk');
    if (!inputKk.dataset.manualEdited) {
      inputKk.value = val;
    }
  }

  document.getElementById('input-nama-kk').addEventListener('input', function() {
    this.dataset.manualEdited = "true";
  });

  function setPilihanUtama(warna) {
    pilihanUtama = warna;
    const btnHijau = document.getElementById('btn-utama-hijau');
    const btnKuning = document.getElementById('btn-utama-kuning');
    const btnMerah = document.getElementById('btn-utama-merah');
    const checkHijau = document.getElementById('check-utama-hijau');
    const checkKuning = document.getElementById('check-utama-kuning');
    const checkMerah = document.getElementById('check-utama-merah');
    const dropdownLawan = document.getElementById('dropdown-lawan-utama');

    btnHijau.className = "min-h-[52px] w-full px-4 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-between transition-all shadow-sm bg-surface text-on-surface-variant opacity-80";
    btnKuning.className = "min-h-[52px] w-full px-4 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-between transition-all shadow-sm bg-surface text-on-surface-variant opacity-80";
    btnMerah.className = "min-h-[52px] w-full px-4 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-between transition-all shadow-sm bg-surface text-on-surface-variant opacity-80";

    checkHijau.textContent = "radio_button_unchecked";
    checkHijau.className = "material-symbols-outlined text-[24px] opacity-30";
    checkKuning.textContent = "radio_button_unchecked";
    checkKuning.className = "material-symbols-outlined text-[24px] opacity-30";
    checkMerah.textContent = "radio_button_unchecked";
    checkMerah.className = "material-symbols-outlined text-[24px] opacity-30";

    dropdownLawan.classList.add('hidden');

    if (warna === 'hijau') {
      btnHijau.className = "min-h-[52px] w-full px-4 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-between transition-all shadow-sm bg-status-kita-bg text-status-kita-text";
      checkHijau.textContent = "check_circle";
      checkHijau.className = "material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]";
    } else if (warna === 'kuning') {
      btnKuning.className = "min-h-[52px] w-full px-4 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-between transition-all shadow-sm bg-status-ragu-bg text-status-ragu-text";
      checkKuning.textContent = "check_circle";
      checkKuning.className = "material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]";
    } else if (warna === 'merah') {
      btnMerah.className = "min-h-[52px] w-full px-4 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-between transition-all shadow-sm bg-status-lain-bg text-status-lain-text";
      checkMerah.textContent = "check_circle";
      checkMerah.className = "material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]";
      dropdownLawan.classList.remove('hidden');
    }
  }

  function tambahAnggotaKeluarga() {
    jumlahKeluarga++;
    const id = 'anggota-' + Date.now();
    const container = document.getElementById('container-keluarga');

    const card = document.createElement('div');
    card.id = id;
    card.className = "p-3 bg-surface-container-low rounded-xl flex flex-col gap-2 relative shadow-sm";
    
    card.innerHTML = `
      <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
        <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <span class="w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-secondary text-on-secondary flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-[11px]">${container.children.length + 1}</span>
          <span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface font-bold">Anggota Tambahan (Satu KK)</span>
        </div>
        <button type="button" onclick="hapusAnggota('${id}')" class="text-error flex items-center p-1">
          <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]">delete</span>
        </button>
      </div>
      <div class="flex gap-2">
        <input type="text" placeholder="Nama Istri / Anak" class="w-full h-10 px-3 bg-surface-card rounded-lg font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-primary shadow-sm" />
        <select class="h-10 px-2 bg-surface-card rounded-lg font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface focus:outline-none shadow-sm">
          <option>Istri</option>
          <option>Anak</option>
          <option>Orang Tua</option>
          <option>Famili Lain</option>
        </select>
      </div>
      <div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-1.5 pt-1">
        <button type="button" onclick="setPilihanAnggota('${id}', 'hijau')" class="btn-pil-hijau h-9 rounded-lg bg-status-kita-bg text-status-kita-text font-label-sm text-label-sm font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-1 shadow-sm">
          <span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-status-kita-text"></span> Hijau
        </button>
        <button type="button" onclick="setPilihanAnggota('${id}', 'kuning')" class="btn-pil-kuning h-9 rounded-lg bg-surface-card text-on-surface-variant font-label-sm text-label-sm opacity-75 flex items-center justify-center gap-1 shadow-sm">
          <span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-status-ragu-text"></span> Kuning
        </button>
        <button type="button" onclick="setPilihanAnggota('${id}', 'merah')" class="btn-pil-merah h-9 rounded-lg bg-surface-card text-on-surface-variant font-label-sm text-label-sm opacity-75 flex items-center justify-center gap-1 shadow-sm">
          <span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-status-lain-text"></span> Merah
        </button>
      </div>
    `;

    container.appendChild(card);
    updateTotalKeluarga();
  }

  function hapusAnggota(id) {
    const el = document.getElementById(id);
    if (el) {
      el.remove();
      updateTotalKeluarga();
    }
  }

  function updateTotalKeluarga() {
    const count = document.getElementById('container-keluarga').children.length + 1;
    document.getElementById('badge-total-keluarga').textContent = count + ' Orang';
  }

  function setPilihanAnggota(cardId, warna) {
    const card = document.getElementById(cardId);
    if (!card) return;

    const bHijau = card.querySelector('.btn-pil-hijau');
    const bKuning = card.querySelector('.btn-pil-kuning');
    const bMerah = card.querySelector('.btn-pil-merah');

    bHijau.className = "btn-pil-hijau h-9 rounded-lg bg-surface-card text-on-surface-variant font-label-sm text-label-sm opacity-75 flex items-center justify-center gap-1 shadow-sm";
    bKuning.className = "btn-pil-kuning h-9 rounded-lg bg-surface-card text-on-surface-variant font-label-sm text-label-sm opacity-75 flex items-center justify-center gap-1 shadow-sm";
    bMerah.className = "btn-pil-merah h-9 rounded-lg bg-surface-card text-on-surface-variant font-label-sm text-label-sm opacity-75 flex items-center justify-center gap-1 shadow-sm";

    if (warna === 'hijau') {
      bHijau.className = "btn-pil-hijau h-9 rounded-lg bg-status-kita-bg text-status-kita-text font-label-sm text-label-sm font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-1 shadow-sm";
    } else if (warna === 'kuning') {
      bKuning.className = "btn-pil-kuning h-9 rounded-lg bg-status-ragu-bg text-status-ragu-text font-label-sm text-label-sm font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-1 shadow-sm";
    } else if (warna === 'merah') {
      bMerah.className = "btn-pil-merah h-9 rounded-lg bg-status-lain-bg text-status-lain-text font-label-sm text-label-sm font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-1 shadow-sm";
    }
  }

  function simpanData() {
    const namaUtama = document.getElementById('input-nama-utama').value.trim();
    if (!namaUtama) {
      document.getElementById('input-nama-utama').focus();
      return;
    }

    const toast = document.getElementById('toast-sukses');
    toast.classList.remove('-translate-y-24');
    toast.classList.add('translate-y-0');

    setTimeout(() => {
      toast.classList.remove('translate-y-0');
      toast.classList.add('-translate-y-24');
      document.getElementById('input-nama-utama').value = '';
      document.getElementById('input-nama-kk').value = '';
      document.getElementById('input-wa').value = '';
      document.getElementById('catatan-lapangan').value = '';
      document.getElementById('container-keluarga').innerHTML = '';
      setPilihanUtama('hijau');
      updateTotalKeluarga();
    }, 1800);
  }
</script></main><nav class="fixed bottom-0 w-full z-50 pb-safe bg-surface/95 backdrop-blur-xl shadow-[0_-2px_12px_rgba(0,0,0,0.06)]" data-active-classes="text-primary font-label-md"><div class="flex items-center justify-around h-16 px-space-xs"><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-w-[64px] min-h-[44px] py-1 text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface transition-colors" data-path="beranda-setoran" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">how_to_vote</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm mt-0.5">Setoran</span></a><a aria-current="page" class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-w-[64px] min-h-[44px] py-1 transition-colors text-primary font-label-md" data-path="input-cepat-warga" href="#"><div class="w-10 h-10 -mt-3 rounded-full bg-primary-container text-on-primary flex items-center justify-center shadow-[0_2px_8px_rgba(21,128,61,0.35)]"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">person_add</span></div><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm mt-0.5">Input</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-w-[64px] min-h-[44px] py-1 text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface transition-colors" data-path="ploting-target" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">ads_click</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm mt-0.5">Target</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-w-[64px] min-h-[44px] py-1 text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface transition-colors" data-path="akun-timses" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">badge</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm mt-0.5">Akun</span></a></div></nav></body></html>

<!-- MY TPS - Dashboard Saksi TPS Ala Instagram -->
<!DOCTYPE html>

<html lang="id"><head><meta charset="utf-8"/><meta content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0, maximum-scale=1.0, user-scalable=no, viewport-fit=cover" name="viewport"/><meta content="mobile_tab" name="shell-type"/><link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@400;600;700&amp;display=swap" rel="stylesheet"/><link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Material+Symbols+Outlined:opsz,wght,FILL,GRAD@20..48,100..700,0..1,-50..200" rel="stylesheet"/>
<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Material+Symbols+Outlined:wght,FILL@100..700,0..1&amp;display=swap" rel="stylesheet"/><style>@layer src="https://cdn.tailwindcss.com"></script><script </div>

<script>
  function markVoted(button) {
    const card = button.closest('article');
    const badge = card.querySelector('.bg-status-ragu-bg');
    if (badge) {
      badge.className = 'shrink-0 px-2 py-0.5 bg-status-kita-bg text-status-kita-text rounded-full font-label-sm text-label-sm leading-none flex items-center gap-0.5';
      badge.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[12px]">check</span><span>Sudah Coblos</span>';
    }
    const actionsWrapper = button.parentElement;
    actionsWrapper.innerHTML = `
      <div class="w-9 h-9 rounded-full bg-status-kita-bg text-status-kita-text flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 animate-scale">
        <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]" style="font-variation-settings: 'FILL' 1;">check_circle</span>
      </div>
    `;
    card.classList.add('opacity-90');
  }
</script></main><nav class="fixed bottom-0 inset-x-0 z-50 pb-safe bg-surface-card/95 backdrop-blur-xl shadow-[0_-1px_8px_rgba(0,0,0,0.04)]" data-active-classes="text-primary font-bold"><div class="flex justify-around items-center h-16 px-gutter-mobile"><a aria-current="page" class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-w-[44px] min-h-[44px] w-16 h-full transition-colors text-primary font-bold" data-path="absensi" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">fact_check</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm mt-0.5">Absensi</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-w-[44px] min-h-[44px] w-16 h-full text-on-surface-variant transition-colors hover:text-on-surface" data-path="ceklis" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">how_to_reg</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm mt-0.5">Ceklis</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-w-[44px] min-h-[44px] w-16 h-full text-on-surface-variant transition-colors hover:text-on-surface" data-path="hitung" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">calculate</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm mt-0.5">Hitung</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-w-[44px] min-h-[44px] w-16 h-full text-on-surface-variant transition-colors hover:text-on-surface" data-path="akun" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">person</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm mt-0.5">Akun</span></a></div></nav></body></html>

<span>•</span>

  // Live Clock updater for operational tactical accuracy
  (function updateLiveTime() {
    const el = document.getElementById('liveClock');
    if (!el) return;
    const now = new Date();
    const hours = String(now.getHours()).padStart(2, '0');
    const minutes = String(now.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0');
    const seconds = String(now.getSeconds()).padStart(2, '0');
    el.textContent = `${hours}:${minutes}:${seconds} WIB`;
    setTimeout(updateLiveTime, 1000);
  })();
</script></main></div></body></html>

mt-space-xs bg-primary text-on-primary rounded-lg font-label-md text-label-md active:bg-primary-dark transition-colors" id="resetFilterBtn" type="button">Reset Filter</button></div><div class="h-6"></div></div>
</div>
<script>
  (function() {
    const searchInput = document.getElementById('voterSearchInput');
    const tabs = document.querySelectorAll('.filter-tab');
    const cards = document.querySelectorAll('.voter-card');
    const emptyState = document.getElementById('voterEmptyState');
    const resetBtn = document.getElementById('resetFilterBtn');

    let currentFilter = 'all';

    function applyFiltering() {
      const query = (searchInput.value || '').toLowerCase().trim();
      let visibleCount = 0;

      cards.forEach(card => {
        const cardStatus = card.getAttribute('data-status');
        const cardSearch = (card.getAttribute('data-search') || '').toLowerCase();

        const matchesStatus = (currentFilter === 'all' || cardStatus === currentFilter);
        const matchesQuery = (!query || cardSearch.includes(query));

        if (matchesStatus && matchesQuery) {
          card.classList.remove('hidden');
          card.classList.add('flex');
          visibleCount++;
        } else {
          card.classList.remove('flex');
          card.classList.add('hidden');
        }
      });

      if (visibleCount === 0) {
        emptyState.classList.remove('hidden');
        emptyState.classList.add('flex');
      } else {
        emptyState.classList.remove('flex');
        emptyState.classList.add('hidden');
      }
    }

    tabs.forEach(tab => {
      tab.addEventListener('click', function() {
        tabs.forEach(t => {
          t.classList.remove('bg-primary', 'text-on-primary');
          t.classList.add('bg-surface-card');
          if (t.dataset.filter === 'hijau') t.classList.add('text-status-kita-text');
          if (t.dataset.filter === 'kuning') t.classList.add('text-status-ragu-text');
          if (t.dataset.filter === 'merah') t.classList.add('text-status-lain-text');
          if (t.dataset.filter === 'all') t.classList.add('text-on-surface');
        });

        this.classList.remove('bg-surface-card', 'text-status-kita-text', 'text-status-ragu-text', 'text-status-lain-text', 'text-on-surface');
        this.classList.add('bg-primary', 'text-on-primary');

        currentFilter = this.getAttribute('data-filter');
        applyFiltering();
      });
    });

    if (searchInput) {
      searchInput.addEventListener('input', applyFiltering);
    }

    if (resetBtn) {
      resetBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
        searchInput.value = '';
        const allTab = document.querySelector('[data-filter="all"]');
        if (allTab) allTab.click();
      });
    }
  })();

<span>Broadcast Hasil Pleno</span>
</button>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-inverse-surface/60 backdrop-blur-sm hidden items-center justify-center p-4" id="planoModal">
<div class="bg-surface-card rounded-2xl max-w-3xl w-full overflow-hidden shadow-2xl flex flex-col">
<div class="p-space-md bg-surface-container-low flex items-center justify-between">
<div class="flex items-center gap-space-sm">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-primary-container text-[24px]">verified</span>
<div>
<h4 class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface" id="modalTitle">Formulir C-Hasil Plano</h4>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-secondary" id="modalSub">Hasil Verifikasi Saksi Terpadu</p>
</div>
</div>
<button class="w-9 h-9 rounded-full bg-surface-container hover:bg-surface-variant flex items-center justify-center text-on-surface" onclick="closePlanoModal()" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]">close</span>
</button>
</div>
<div class="p-space-md flex flex-col gap-space-md">
<div class="w-full h-80 bg-surface-container rounded-xl overflow-hidden relative">
<img class="w-full h-full object-cover" data-alt="High resolution detailed macro photograph of the official C-Plano election document with handwritten tallies, witness stamps in violet ink, official village election council signature, and sharp paper texture." src="https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/aida-public/AB6AXuCm9CN1hamjzLtVOc-8OqhBVsFt1TnQ63ycFE9ONnO3NUaO-tnggM_kA_PPF0qu-mchMO2C0WaQr6yxqb_7bClh0_3aU4mkiRWq2nfVze9uell7RaJU7gL2NeAA-ImEyvXgcwokcjfkrGbjIRbEVLOjTFTTcuxuYJYiv4cj4u8MmAzzxBm-EjTFXzA5-ABw4IcufyaCspBq-RH9hf8Q8TBs0pg4HWaB0YaDBPN2XLGs"/>
<div class="absolute bottom-3 right-3 bg-surface-card/90 backdrop-blur px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-label-sm font-label-sm text-on-surface shadow-sm">
            Resolusi: 4032 x 3024px (Original Scan)
          </div>
</div>
<div class="flex items-center justify-between text-body-sm font-body-sm bg-status-kita-bg p-space-sm rounded-lg text-status-kita-text">
<span class="flex items-center gap-1 font-semibold">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]">verified</span> Stempel Panitia TPS Terverifikasi Otentik
          </span>
<span class="text-on-surface-variant font-medium">Watermark Hash: #PLK-SKM-2024-C1</span>
</div>
</div>
<div class="p-space-md bg-surface-container-low flex items-center justify-end gap-space-sm">
<button class="h-10 px-space-md bg-surface text-on-surface rounded-lg font-label-md text-label-md flex items-center gap-1 hover:bg-surface-container" onclick="alert('Mengunduh file RAW Plano beresolusi tinggi...')" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]">file_download</span> Unduh Asli
        </button>
<button class="h-10 px-space-md bg-primary-container text-on-primary rounded-lg font-label-md text-label-md hover:bg-primary-dark" onclick="closePlanoModal()" type="button">
          Tutup Pratinjau
        </button>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<script>
    function showPlanoModal(title, details) {
      const modal = document.getElementById('planoModal');
      document.getElementById('modalTitle').innerText = 'Foto C-Plano Fisik: ' + title;
      document.getElementById('modalSub').innerText = details;
      modal.classList.remove('hidden');
      modal.classList.add('flex');
    }

    function closePlanoModal() {
      const modal = document.getElementById('planoModal');
      modal.classList.remove('flex');
      modal.classList.add('hidden');
    }
  </script>
</div></main></div></body></html>

</div>
<h1 class="font-headline-lg text-headline-lg text-on-surface tracking-tight mt-1">

</div>

<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-space-sm">

</div>

</div>

<script>
  // Simple filter tab interactivity for territory views
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
    const buttons = ['filter-all', 'filter-d1', 'filter-d2', 'filter-d3'];
    buttons.forEach(id => {
      const el = document.getElementById(id);
      if (el) {
        el.addEventListener('click', () => {
          buttons.forEach(otherId => {
            const otherEl = document.getElementById(otherId);
            if (otherEl) {
              otherEl.classList.remove('bg-surface-card', 'text-primary', 'shadow-sm');
              otherEl.classList.add('text-secondary');
            }
          });
          el.classList.remove('text-secondary');
          el.classList.add('bg-surface-card', 'text-primary', 'shadow-sm');
        });
      }
    });
  });
</script></main></div></body></html>

<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface">Keluarga Bpk. Slamet Riyadi (3 DPT) sepakat mendukung Paslon 01 pasca kunjungan silaturahmi relawan desa.</p>
<div class="flex items-center gap-1 font-label-sm text-label-sm text-secondary mt-1">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[14px]">account_circle</span>
<span>Pencatat: Saksi TPS 04 (Budi Gunawan)</span>
</div>
</div>
<div class="p-space-sm rounded-lg bg-surface-container-low flex flex-col gap-1 transition-all hover:bg-surface-container">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between">
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-primary font-bold">Dusun III / RT 05 (TPS 11)</span>
<span class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-secondary">28 mnt lalu</span>
</div>
<div class="flex items-center gap-2 my-0.5">
<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-label-sm text-label-sm bg-status-lain-bg text-status-lain-text">Pilih Calon Lain</span>
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] text-secondary">arrow_forward</span>
<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-label-sm text-label-sm bg-status-ragu-bg text-status-ragu-text">Masih Ragu</span>
</div>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface">Ibu Warsini menangguhkan pilihan ke 02 karena simpati pada program kesehatan gratis paslon 01.</p>
<div class="flex items-center gap-1 font-label-sm text-label-sm text-secondary mt-1">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[14px]">account_circle</span>
<span>Pencatat: Kordinator Dusun (Siti Nurhaliza)</span>
</div>
</div>
<div class="p-space-sm rounded-lg bg-surface-container-low flex flex-col gap-1 transition-all hover:bg-surface-container">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between">
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-primary font-bold">Dusun II / RT 01 (TPS 07)</span>
<span class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-secondary">1 jam lalu</span>
</div>
<div class="flex items-center gap-2 my-0.5">
<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-label-sm text-label-sm bg-status-ragu-bg text-status-ragu-text">Masih Ragu</span>
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] text-secondary">arrow_forward</span>
<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-label-sm text-label-sm bg-status-kita-bg text-status-kita-text">Pemilih Kita</span>
</div>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface">Komunitas pedagang pasar sore (14 pedagang terdata) menyatakan deklarasi komitmen bersama.</p>
<div class="flex items-center gap-1 font-label-sm text-label-sm text-secondary mt-1">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[14px]">account_circle</span>
<span>Pencatat: Tim Relawan Pasar (Ahmad Fauzi)</span>
</div>
</div>
<div class="p-space-sm rounded-lg bg-surface-container-low flex flex-col gap-1 transition-all hover:bg-surface-container">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between">
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-primary font-bold">Dusun IV / RT 03 (TPS 15)</span>
<span class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-secondary">2 jam lalu</span>
</div>
<div class="flex items-center gap-2 my-0.5">
<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-label-sm text-label-sm bg-status-kita-bg text-status-kita-text">Pemilih Kita</span>
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] text-status-kita-text">verified</span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-status-kita-text font-bold">Konfirmasi Ulang</span>
</div>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface">Validasi NIK DPT keluarga pensiunan guru selesai. Seluruh 4 anggota keluarga siap hadir pukul 08:30 WIB.</p>
<div class="flex items-center gap-1 font-label-sm text-label-sm text-secondary mt-1">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[14px]">account_circle</span>
<span>Pencatat: Saksi TPS 15 (Dewi Sartika)</span>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<button class="w-full py-2.5 bg-surface-container hover:bg-surface-container-high text-on-surface rounded-lg font-label-md text-label-md flex items-center justify-center gap-1 transition-colors" type="button">
<span>Lihat Semua 142 Log Masuk</span>
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]">keyboard_arrow_down</span>
</button>
</div>
<div class="bg-surface-card rounded-xl p-space-lg shadow-sm flex flex-col gap-space-sm">
<div class="flex items-center gap-space-xs text-on-surface">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px] text-tertiary">notification_important</span>
<h2 class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm">Rekomendasi Taktis Hari Ini</h2>
</div>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-secondary">Analitik AI sistem menyarankan tindakan cepat pada titik krusial sebelum masa tenang:</p>
<ul class="flex flex-col gap-2 pt-1">
<li class="p-2 rounded bg-surface-container-low flex items-start gap-2">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-status-ragu-text text-[18px] shrink-0 mt-0.5">campaign</span>
<span class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface">Fokuskan armada pengantar lansia ke TPS 11 dan TPS 13 Dusun Tegalsari untuk mengamankan 28 swing voter lansia.</span>
</li>
<li class="p-2 rounded bg-surface-container-low flex items-start gap-2">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-primary-container text-[18px] shrink-0 mt-0.5">verified_user</span>
<span class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface">Pastikan 100% mandat saksi TPS 01 - TPS 20 telah terdistribusi dan terverifikasi di aplikasi Saksi Kita.</span>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<script>
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
      const feedContainer = document.getElementById('feed-container');
      if (!feedContainer) return;

      const dummyUpdates = [
        {
          loc: 'Dusun I / RT 04 (TPS 02)',
          time: 'Baru saja',
          from: 'Masih Ragu',
          fromBg: 'bg-status-ragu-bg',
          fromText: 'text-status-ragu-text',
          to: 'Pemilih Kita',
          toBg: 'bg-status-kita-bg',
          toText: 'text-status-kita-text',
          msg: 'Bpk. Hendro & Istri memutuskan mendukung 01 usai klarifikasi isu bansos lokal.',
          user: 'Relawan Dusun I (Agus Prayitno)'
        }
      ];

      setInterval(() => {
        const item = dummyUpdates[0];
        const card = document.createElement('div');
        card.className = 'p-space-sm rounded-lg bg-surface-container-low flex flex-col gap-1 transition-all opacity-0 translate-y-2';
        card.innerHTML = `
          <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-primary font-bold">\${item.loc}</span>
            <span class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-secondary">\${item.time}</span>
          </div>
          <div class="flex items-center gap-2 my-0.5">
            <span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-label-sm text-label-sm \${item.fromBg} \${item.fromText}">\${item.from}</span>
            <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] text-secondary">arrow_forward</span>
            <span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-label-sm text-label-sm \${item.toBg} \${item.toText}">\${item.to}</span>
          </div>
          <p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface">\${item.msg}</p>
          <div class="flex items-center gap-1 font-label-sm text-label-sm text-secondary mt-1">
            <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[14px]">account_circle</span>
            <span>Pencatat: \${item.user}</span>
          </div>
        `;
        if (feedContainer.firstChild) {
          feedContainer.insertBefore(card, feedContainer.firstChild);
          setTimeout(() => {
            card.classList.remove('opacity-0', 'translate-y-2');
            card.classList.add('opacity-100', 'translate-y-0');
          }, 50);
          if (feedContainer.children.length > 5) {
            feedContainer.removeChild(feedContainer.lastChild);
          }
        }
      }, 15000);
    });
  </script>
</div></main></div></body></html>

<!-- Top Command & Title Bar -->
<div class="flex flex-col lg:flex-row lg:items-center justify-between gap-space-md bg-surface-card rounded-xl p-space-lg shadow-sm">
<div class="flex items-start sm:items-center gap-space-md">
<div class="w-12 h-12 rounded-xl bg-status-kita-bg flex items-center justify-center text-status-kita-text shrink-0">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[28px]">badge</span>
</div>
<div>
<div class="flex items-center gap-space-xs flex-wrap">
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-secondary uppercase tracking-wider">Teritorial Desa Terverifikasi</span>
<span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-secondary"></span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-primary-container font-semibold">SK-KPU: 042/KMJ/2024</span>
</div>
<h1 class="font-headline-md text-headline-md text-on-surface tracking-tight mt-0.5">

          </h1>
</div>
</div>
<div class="flex items-center gap-space-sm self-start lg:self-center shrink-0">

</div>

<div class="flex items-center justify-between">
