</span>
</div>
<div class="mt-space-md">
<div class="flex items-baseline gap-space-xs">
<span class="font-headline-lg text-headline-lg text-on-surface">810</span>
<span class="font-label-md text-label-md text-secondary">Pemilih</span>
</div>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-secondary mt-1">4 TPS terdaftar di Kampung Makmur Jaya</p>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Metric 2 -->
<div class="bg-surface-card rounded-xl p-space-lg shadow-sm flex flex-col justify-between">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between">
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-secondary uppercase">Saksi Bertugas Mandat</span>
<span class="p-2 rounded-lg bg-status-kita-bg text-status-kita-text">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]">assignment_ind</span>
</span>
</div>
<div class="mt-space-md">
<div class="flex items-baseline gap-space-xs">
<span class="font-headline-lg text-headline-lg text-status-kita-text">8 / 8</span>
<span class="font-label-md text-label-md text-secondary">Saksi (4 TPS)</span>
</div>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-secondary mt-1">Formulir mandat fisik & digital tercetak</p>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Metric 3 -->
<div class="bg-surface-card rounded-xl p-space-lg shadow-sm flex flex-col justify-between">

<!-- MODAL: Tambah Saksi TPS (Interactive Pop-up) -->
<div class="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-[#0F172A]/40 flex items-center justify-center p-4 hidden" id="modalSaksi">
<div class="bg-surface-card rounded-xl max-w-lg w-full p-space-lg shadow-xl flex flex-col gap-space-md">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between pb-space-sm">
<div class="flex items-center gap-space-sm">
<div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-lg bg-status-kita-bg text-status-kita-text flex items-center justify-center">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">person_add</span>
</div>
<div>
<h3 class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface">Penerbitan Mandat Saksi</h3>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-secondary">Daftarkan saksi resmi bertugas di TPS</p>
</div>
</div>
<button class="w-9 h-9 rounded-lg bg-surface-container text-secondary hover:text-on-surface flex items-center justify-center" id="btnCloseModal" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]">close</span>
</button>
</div>
<form class="space-y-space-md" id="formAddSaksi" onsubmit="event.preventDefault();">
<div>
<label class="block font-label-sm text-label-sm text-secondary uppercase mb-1">Nama Lengkap Sesuai KTP</label>
<input class="w-full h-11 px-3 bg-surface-container rounded-lg font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-container" placeholder="cth. Muhammad Ridwan" required="" type="text"/>
</div>
<div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-space-md">
<div>
<label class="block font-label-sm text-label-sm text-secondary uppercase mb-1">Nomor WhatsApp</label>
<input class="w-full h-11 px-3 bg-surface-container rounded-lg font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-container" placeholder="0812xxxxxxx" required="" type="tel"/>
</div>
<div>
<label class="block font-label-sm text-label-sm text-secondary uppercase mb-1">Penugasan TPS</label>
<select class="w-full h-11 px-3 bg-surface-container rounded-lg font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-container">
<option value="TPS 01">TPS 01</option>
<option value="TPS 02">TPS 02</option>
<option value="TPS 03">TPS 03</option>
<option value="TPS 04">TPS 04</option>
</select>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<label class="block font-label-sm text-label-sm text-secondary uppercase mb-1">Status Peran Saksi</label>
<div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-space-sm">
<label class="flex items-center gap-2 p-2.5 bg-surface-container rounded-lg cursor-pointer">
<input checked="" class="accent-primary-container" name="peran" type="radio" value="utama"/>
<span class="font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface">Saksi Inti / Utama</span>
</label>
<label class="flex items-center gap-2 p-2.5 bg-surface-container rounded-lg cursor-pointer">
<input class="accent-primary-container" name="peran" type="radio" value="cadangan"/>
<span class="font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface">Saksi Cadangan</span>
</label>
</div>
</div>
<div class="pt-space-sm flex justify-end gap-space-sm">
<button class="h-11 px-space-md bg-surface-container text-on-surface rounded-lg font-label-md text-label-md hover:bg-surface-dim transition-colors" id="btnCancelModal" type="button">
            Batal
          </button>
<button class="h-11 px-space-md bg-primary-container text-on-primary rounded-lg font-label-md text-label-md hover:bg-primary-dark transition-colors flex items-center gap-1.5" id="btnSubmitSaksi" type="submit">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]">verified</span>
<span>Terbitkan Mandat & QR</span>
</button>
</div>
</form>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Toast Notifikasi Ringan -->
<div class="fixed bottom-6 right-6 z-50 bg-inverse-surface text-inverse-on-surface px-space-md py-3 rounded-xl shadow-lg flex items-center gap-space-sm opacity-0 pointer-events-none transition-all duration-300" id="toastMessage">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px] text-inverse-primary" id="toastIcon">check_circle</span>
<span class="font-label-md text-label-md" id="toastText">Operasi berhasil dijalankan</span>
</div>
<!-- Client-Side Behavior -->
<script>
    (function() {
      // Filter Elements
      const filterDusun = document.getElementById('filterDusun');
      const filterTPS = document.getElementById('filterTPS');
      const filterKehadiran = document.getElementById('filterKehadiran');
      const filterDukungan = document.getElementById('filterDukungan');
      const searchInput = document.getElementById('searchInput');
      const rows = document.querySelectorAll('.voter-row');
      const displayedCount = document.getElementById('displayedCount');

      function filterTable() {
        const dVal = filterDusun.value;
        const tVal = filterTPS.value;
        const kVal = filterKehadiran.value;
        const dukVal = filterDukungan.value;
        const sVal = searchInput.value.toLowerCase().trim();

        let visible = 0;

        rows.forEach(row => {
          const dusun = row.getAttribute('data-dusun');
          const tps = row.getAttribute('data-tps');
          const kehadiran = row.getAttribute('data-kehadiran');
          const dukungan = row.getAttribute('data-dukungan');
          const textContent = row.textContent.toLowerCase();

          const matchDusun = (dVal === 'all' || dusun === dVal);
          const matchTPS = (tVal === 'all' || tps === tVal);
          const matchKehadiran = (kVal === 'all' || kehadiran === kVal);
          const matchDukungan = (dukVal === 'all' || dukungan === dukVal);
          const matchSearch = (!sVal || textContent.includes(sVal));

          if (matchDusun && matchTPS && matchKehadiran && matchDukungan && matchSearch) {
            row.style.display = '';
            visible++;
          } else {
            row.style.display = 'none';
          }
        });

        if (displayedCount) {
          displayedCount.textContent = visible;
        }
      }

      [filterDusun, filterTPS, filterKehadiran, filterDukungan].forEach(sel => {
        sel.addEventListener('change', filterTable);
      });
      searchInput.addEventListener('input', filterTable);

      // Toast Utility
      function showToast(msg, icon = 'check_circle') {
        const toast = document.getElementById('toastMessage');
        const toastText = document.getElementById('toastText');
        const toastIcon = document.getElementById('toastIcon');
        if (!toast) return;

        toastText.textContent = msg;
        toastIcon.textContent = icon;
        toast.classList.remove('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        toast.classList.add('opacity-100');

        setTimeout(() => {
          toast.classList.remove('opacity-100');
          toast.classList.add('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        }, 3000);
      }

      // Check Duplicates Trigger
      const btnCheckDuplicate = document.getElementById('btnCheckDuplicate');
      if (btnCheckDuplicate) {
        btnCheckDuplicate.addEventListener('click', () => {
          btnCheckDuplicate.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px] animate-spin">refresh</span><span>Memeriksa...</span>';
          setTimeout(() => {
            btnCheckDuplicate.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px] text-secondary">verified_user</span><span>Cek Duplikasi DPT</span>';
            showToast('Pemeriksaan selesai: 0 Duplikasi NIK atau Nama ganda ditemukan.', 'verified');
          }, 800);
        });
      }

      // Modal Controls
      const modalSaksi = document.getElementById('modalSaksi');
      const btnAddSaksi = document.getElementById('btnAddSaksi');
      const btnCloseModal = document.getElementById('btnCloseModal');
      const btnCancelModal = document.getElementById('btnCancelModal');
      const formAddSaksi = document.getElementById('formAddSaksi');

      function openModal() {
        modalSaksi.classList.remove('hidden');
      }

      function closeModal() {
        modalSaksi.classList.add('hidden');
      }

      if (btnAddSaksi) btnAddSaksi.addEventListener('click', openModal);
      if (btnCloseModal) btnCloseModal.addEventListener('click', closeModal);
      if (btnCancelModal) btnCancelModal.addEventListener('click', closeModal);

      if (formAddSaksi) {
        formAddSaksi.addEventListener('submit', (e) => {
          e.preventDefault();
          closeModal();
          showToast('Mandat Saksi baru berhasil diterbitkan & QR terdaftar.');
        });
      }

      // Plot Action Buttons
      const plotButtons = document.querySelectorAll('.btn-plot');
      plotButtons.forEach(btn => {
        btn.addEventListener('click', function() {
          const row = this.closest('tr');
          const voterName = row ? row.querySelector('.font-label-md').textContent : 'Pemilih';
          showToast(`Ploting cepat untuk ${voterName} disesuaikan ke data TPS.`);
        });
      });

      // Export Button
      const btnExport = document.getElementById('btnExport');
      if (btnExport) {
        btnExport.addEventListener('click', () => {
          showToast('Menyiapkan berkas Excel rekap DPT & Saksi...');
        });
      }
    })();
  </script>
</div></main></div></body></html>

<!-- Master Konfigurasi & Kendali Admin -->

<html lang="en"><head><meta charset="utf-8"/><meta content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0" name="viewport"/><meta content="web_dashboard" name="shell-type"/><link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Material+Symbols+Outlined:opsz,wght,FILL,GRAD@20..48,100..700,0..1,-50..200" rel="stylesheet"/>

<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@100..900&amp;display=swap" rel="stylesheet"/>
<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Material+Symbols+Outlined:wght,FILL@100..700,0..1&amp;display=swap" rel="stylesheet"/><style>@layer text-[18px]">person</span></div></div></header><main class="relative pt-16 w-full min-h-screen bg-bg-base px-space-xl py-space-xl"><div class="flex flex-col w-full">
<!-- Top Command Banner -->
<div class="flex flex-col lg:flex-row lg:items-center lg:justify-between gap-space-lg mb-space-xl">
<div>
<div class="inline-flex items-center gap-space-xs px-space-sm py-1 rounded bg-status-kita-bg text-status-kita-text font-label-sm text-label-sm mb-space-xs">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px]">verified_user</span>
<span>HAK AKSES KHUSUS • MASTER ADMIN</span>
</div>
<h1 class="font-headline-lg text-headline-lg text-on-surface tracking-tight">Master Konfigurasi, Manajemen Wilayah &amp; Akun Tim</h1>
<p class="font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface-variant mt-1">Pusat kendali pengaturan basis data calon, penetapan target menang per dusun, alokasi TPS, registrasi akun timses/saksi, dan nomor darurat WA.</p>
</div>
<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-space-sm">
<button class="flex items-center gap-space-xs h-11 px-space-md rounded-lg bg-surface-card text-on-surface font-label-md text-label-md hover:bg-surface-container transition-colors shadow-sm" onclick="exportConfigJson()">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]">download</span>
<span>Ekspor Cadangan (JSON)</span>
</button>
<button class="flex items-center gap-space-xs h-11 px-space-md rounded-lg bg-surface-card text-on-surface font-label-md text-label-md hover:bg-surface-container transition-colors shadow-sm" onclick="toggleModal('modal-add-user')">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]">person_add</span>
<span>+ Tambah Akun Baru</span>
</button>
<button class="flex items-center gap-space-xs h-11 px-space-lg rounded-lg bg-primary-container text-on-primary font-label-md text-label-md hover:bg-primary-dark transition-colors shadow-sm" onclick="triggerToast('Perubahan master konfigurasi tersimpan sukses ke database!')">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]">save</span>
<span>Simpan Perubahan Sistem</span>
</button>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Operational KPI Setup Row -->
<div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-space-lg mb-space-xl">
<div class="bg-surface-card rounded-xl p-space-lg flex items-start gap-space-md shadow-sm">
<div class="w-12 h-12 rounded-lg bg-surface-container-low flex items-center justify-center text-primary-container shrink-0">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[26px]">groups</span>
</div>
<div class="min-w-0 flex-1">
<span class="block font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant uppercase tracking-wider">Total Kandidat</span>
<div class="flex items-baseline gap-space-xs mt-1">
<span class="font-headline-md text-headline-md text-on-surface">3 Paslon</span>
<span class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-primary-container font-medium">1 Calon Utama</span>
</div>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface-variant mt-0.5 truncate">2 Pasangan Penantang Aktif</p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="bg-surface-card rounded-xl p-space-lg flex items-start gap-space-md shadow-sm">
<div class="w-12 h-12 rounded-lg bg-surface-container-low flex items-center justify-center text-primary-container shrink-0">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[26px]">apartment</span>
</div>
<div class="min-w-0 flex-1">
<span class="block font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant uppercase tracking-wider">Dusun Terdaftar</span>
<div class="flex items-baseline gap-space-xs mt-1">
<span class="font-headline-md text-headline-md text-on-surface">3 Dusun</span>
<span class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface-variant">/ 100% Terplot</span>
</div>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-status-kita-text mt-0.5 truncate">Total Target: 650 Suara Menang</p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="bg-surface-card rounded-xl p-space-lg flex items-start gap-space-md shadow-sm">
<div class="w-12 h-12 rounded-lg bg-surface-container-low flex items-center justify-center text-primary-container shrink-0">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[26px]">where_to_vote</span>
</div>
<div class="min-w-0 flex-1">
<span class="block font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant uppercase tracking-wider">TPS Terpasang</span>
<div class="flex items-baseline gap-space-xs mt-1">
<span class="font-headline-md text-headline-md text-on-surface">4 Titik TPS</span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-status-kita-bg text-status-kita-text">Lengkap</span>
</div>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface-variant mt-0.5 truncate">4/4 Saksi Mandat Siap</p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="bg-surface-card rounded-xl p-space-lg flex items-start gap-space-md shadow-sm">
<div class="w-12 h-12 rounded-lg bg-surface-container-low flex items-center justify-center text-primary-container shrink-0">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[26px]">badge</span>
</div>
<div class="min-w-0 flex-1">
<span class="block font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant uppercase tracking-wider">Total Akun Pengguna</span>
<div class="flex items-baseline gap-space-xs mt-1">
<span class="font-headline-md text-headline-md text-on-surface">10 Akun</span>
<span class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-status-kita-text">Semua Aktif</span>
</div>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface-variant mt-0.5 truncate">1 Admin • 3 Koor • 2 Tim • 4 Saksi</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<!-- SECTION 1: MASTER CALON KEPALA KAMPUNG & PENANTANG -->
<div class="bg-surface-card rounded-xl p-space-lg shadow-sm mb-space-xl">
<div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between gap-space-sm mb-space-lg">
<div class="flex items-center gap-space-sm">
<div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-surface-container flex items-center justify-center text-primary-container">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]">how_to_reg</span>
</div>
<div>
<h2 class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface">Master Calon Kepala Kampung &amp; Penantang</h2>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface-variant">Konfigurasi nomor urut, foto resmi surat suara, dan identitas paslon Pilkades.</p>
</div>
</div>
<button class="flex items-center gap-space-xs h-10 px-space-md rounded-lg bg-surface-container-low text-on-surface font-label-md text-label-md hover:bg-surface-container transition-colors" onclick="toggleForm('form-tambah-calon')">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]">add_circle</span>
<span>+ Tambah Calon Baru</span>
</button>
</div>
<!-- Candidate Grid -->
<div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-space-lg mb-space-lg">
<!-- Calon 01: H. Rahmat Hidayat (Kita) -->

<div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-space-sm mb-space-md">

<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[14px]">stars</span>
<span>KANDIDAT KITA</span>
</span>

</div>
<div class="min-w-0">
<h3 class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface leading-tight truncate">H. Rahmat Hidayat</h3>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface-variant mt-0.5">Petahana / Tokoh Masyarakat</p>
<div class="mt-2 inline-flex items-center gap-1 font-label-sm text-label-sm text-status-kita-text">
<span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-status-kita-text"></span>
<span>Status Utama Aktif</span>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="p-space-sm rounded-lg bg-surface-container-low mb-space-md">
<span class="block font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant uppercase">Slogan Kampanye</span>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface italic mt-0.5">“Kader Inklusif, Membangun Desa Mandiri &amp; Sejahtera”</p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="flex items-center justify-between pt-space-sm">
<span class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface-variant">Terdaftar di 4 TPS</span>
<button class="flex items-center gap-1 h-9 px-space-sm rounded-lg text-primary-container font-label-sm text-label-sm hover:bg-surface-container transition-colors" onclick="triggerToast('Membuka modal edit H. Rahmat Hidayat')">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px]">edit</span>
<span>Edit Data</span>
</button>
</div>
</div>

<div class="rounded-xl p-space-lg bg-surface-card shadow-sm flex flex-col justify-between">

<div class="mt-2 inline-flex items-center gap-1 font-label-sm text-label-sm text-secondary">

<span>Peserta Sah KPUD</span>
</div>

<div class="p-space-sm rounded-lg bg-surface-container-low mb-space-md">

<!-- Operational Toast Notification Floating Banner -->
<div class="fixed bottom-6 right-6 z-50 hidden bg-on-surface text-surface-card px-space-lg py-space-md rounded-xl shadow-xl flex items-center gap-space-sm" id="toast-banner">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px] text-status-kita-text">check_circle</span>
<span class="font-body-sm text-body-sm font-medium" id="toast-message">Operasi berhasil dilaksanakan.</span>
</div>
</div>
<script>
  function toggleForm(id) {
    const el = document.getElementById(id);
    if (el) {
      el.classList.toggle('hidden');
    }
  }

  function toggleModal(id) {
    const modal = document.getElementById(id);
    if (modal) {
      modal.classList.toggle('hidden');
    }
  }

  function triggerToast(msg) {
    const toast = document.getElementById('toast-banner');
    const msgEl = document.getElementById('toast-message');
    if (toast && msgEl) {
      msgEl.innerText = msg;
      toast.classList.remove('hidden');
      setTimeout(() => {
        toast.classList.add('hidden');
      }, 3500);
    }
  }

  function copyText(text) {
    navigator.clipboard?.writeText(text).then(() => {
      triggerToast('Kata sandi berhasil disalin ke clipboard: ' + text);
    }).catch(() => {
      triggerToast('Kata sandi disalin: ' + text);
    });
  }

  function sendWaCredentials(name, phone, role) {
    const message = encodeURIComponent(`Halo ${name},\nBerikut kredensial resmi Tim Pemenangan H. Rahmat Hidayat untuk ${role}.\nPortal: https://pilkades-rh.id\nNomor: ${phone}\nMohon jaga kerahasiaan mandat.`);
    window.open(`https://wa.me/${phone.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '')}?text=${message}`, '_blank');
    triggerToast(`Mengirim kredensial via WA ke ${name} (${phone})`);
  }

  function filterUsers() {
    const searchVal = (document.getElementById('user-search')?.value || '').toLowerCase();
    const roleVal = document.getElementById('role-filter')?.value || 'all';
    const rows = document.querySelectorAll('.user-row');

    rows.forEach(row => {
      const text = row.innerText.toLowerCase();
      const role = row.getAttribute('data-role');
      const matchSearch = text.includes(searchVal);
      const matchRole = (roleVal === 'all') || (role === roleVal);

      if (matchSearch && matchRole) {
        row.style.display = '';
      } else {
        row.style.display = 'none';
      }
    });
  }

  function exportConfigJson() {
    const configData = {
      kandidat_utama: "H. Rahmat Hidayat",
      penantang: ["Drs. Bambang Suryono", "Hj. Ratna Sari, S.Pd"],
      total_target: 650,
      dusun: [
        { nama: "Dusun 01 Krajan", target: 250, tps: ["TPS 01", "TPS 02"], koor: "Budi Santoso" },
        { nama: "Dusun 02 Sukamaju", target: 220, tps: ["TPS 03"], koor: "Rina Wati" },
        { nama: "Dusun 03 Harapan", target: 180, tps: ["TPS 04"], koor: "Hendra Gunawan" }
      ],
      whatsapp_helpdesk: document.getElementById('admin-wa-number')?.value || "0812-3456-7890",
      status_db: "Supabase Connected",
      exported_at: new Date().toISOString()
    };
    
    const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(configData, null, 2)], { type: "application/json" });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url;
    a.download = `MasterConfig_PilkadesRH_${new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)}.json`;
    a.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    triggerToast('Cadangan Database JSON berhasil diunduh!');
  }

  function resetTemplate() {
    const template = document.getElementById('wa-template');
    if (template) {
      template.value = `Halo Rekan {NAMA},\n\nBerikut kredensial resmi Akun Saksi/Koor Tim Sukses H. Rahmat Hidayat (Pilkades 2024):\nRole: {ROLE}\nWilayah: {WILAYAH}\nNomor Terdaftar: {NO_WA}\nKata Sandi Sementara: {PASSWORD}\n\nSilakan login di portal resmi dan segera ganti kata sandi setelah masuk.\nBila butuh bantuan teknis, balas pesan ini atau hubungi Hotline HQ.`;
      triggerToast('Template WhatsApp dikembalikan ke standar awal.');
    }
  }

  function testSendWa() {
    const template = document.getElementById('wa-template')?.value || '';
    let preview = template.replace('{NAMA}', 'Budi Santoso')
                          .replace('{ROLE}', 'Koor Dusun')
                          .replace('{WILAYAH}', 'Dusun Krajan')
                          .replace('{NO_WA}', '0852-7712-4433')
                          .replace('{PASSWORD}', 'Krj#Budi01');
    alert('PRATINJAU BROADCAST WHATSAPP:\n\n' + preview);
  }
</script></main></div></body></html>
